原始 task 输入

中文翻译

审查员工 E-443 的 IT 访问申请批次 AR-29。需要根据可用资料判断：哪些权限应该批准，哪些权限应该拒绝以及拒绝原因，请求整体风险等级是多少，在任何已批准权限正式开通前还需要哪些额外审批，如果要批准当前被拒绝的项目需要哪些策略例外，批次里哪些申请不属于本次 AR-29/E-443 审查而应排除，以及应标记哪些安全风险。评估时必须综合使用角色权限矩阵、数据访问策略和访问历史。最终只能返回 JSON 对象，字段包括：approved_permissions、denied_permissions、risk_level、required_approvals、policy_exceptions_needed、excluded_requests、risk_flags。approved_permissions 和 denied_permissions 都应使用简短 snake_case 标签表示资源和权限级别；risk_level 只能是 low、medium 或 high；required_approvals 表示开通已批准权限前还需要的审批角色；policy_exceptions_needed 表示若要批准当前被拒绝项所需的例外，若没有可覆盖的例外则为空；excluded_requests 表示属于其他批次或其他员工、因此不属于本次审查的申请；risk_flags 表示识别出的安全风险。risk_flags 需要列出资料直接支持的每一个独立安全风险原因，而不是只列最高层级风险。JSON 外不要输出解释。

Original instruction

Review IT access request batch AR-29 for employee E-443 using the available sources. Determine which permissions should be approved, which should be denied and why, the overall risk level of this request, what additional approvals are required before any approved permissions can be granted, any policy exceptions that would be needed, which requests in the batch should be excluded from this review, and any security risk flags. Apply the role permission matrix, data access policy, and access history when evaluating each request. Return only a JSON object with these fields: approved_permissions, denied_permissions, risk_level, required_approvals, policy_exceptions_needed, excluded_requests, risk_flags. approved_permissions must be an array of short snake_case labels for resources and permission levels that can be granted under current policy. denied_permissions must be an array of short snake_case labels for resources and permission levels that cannot be granted. risk_level must be a short lowercase string: low, medium, or high. required_approvals must be an array of short snake_case labels for approver roles needed before any approved permissions are activated. policy_exceptions_needed must be an array of short snake_case labels for exceptions that would be required to approve any currently denied item (leave empty if no exceptions could override a denial). excluded_requests must be an array of short snake_case labels for requests in the batch that belong to other batches or other employees and are not part of AR-29. risk_flags must be an array of short snake_case labels for security risks identified. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

data_policy_ar29 data/generated/hard_v1/sources/hard_access_review_ar29/data_policy_ar29.md
# Data Access and Security Policy

## Contractor Access Restrictions

Contractors are treated as external parties and may only access systems listed
as explicitly permitted for the contractor role in the role permission matrix.
Any resource showing "none" in the matrix requires a signed Data Access Exception
(DAE) approved by the Security Lead and the department head.

## PII Access

Customer PII databases are classified as Restricted. Access requires:
1. A business justification approved by the department head
2. Security Lead sign-off
3. No prior data handling violations on record

QA and testing must use anonymized or synthetic data sets, not production PII,
unless a specific production data exception is approved by the Privacy Officer.

## Delete Permissions

Delete-level access to any production database is never granted to contractors
or analysts. This applies regardless of business justification.

## Audit Log Access

Internal audit logs are classified as Confidential. Contractor role access level
is "none" per the role matrix. Debugging access for contractors must be routed
through a supervised session with an on-staff engineer.

## Deployment Pipeline

Write access to the deployment pipeline requires the engineer role or above.
Contractor role does not include deployment write access by default.
role_permission_matrix_ar29 data/generated/hard_v1/sources/hard_access_review_ar29/role_permission_matrix_ar29.csv
role,resource,max_permission_level,requires_additional_approval
employee,usage_reports,read,false
employee,internal_audit_logs,read,false
employee,customer_pii_database,none,true
analyst,financial_reports,read,false
analyst,usage_reports,read,false
engineer,deployment_pipeline,write,false
engineer,usage_reports,read,false
engineer,internal_audit_logs,read,false
contractor,usage_reports,read,false
contractor,internal_audit_logs,none,true
contractor,customer_pii_database,none,true
contractor,deployment_pipeline,none,true
manager,customer_pii_database,read,true

正确解答过程

Only R5 (usage_reports read) is in the contractor role matrix as allowed, and it does not require additional approval. R1 (PII read): contractor role = none, plus prior PII handling violation makes this denied. R2 (audit logs read): contractor role = none, and contractor debugging access should use a supervised session instead of direct access, so it is denied. R3 (deployment write): contractor role = none and write access requires engineer role or above, so it is denied. R4 (PII delete): contractor role = none AND policy states delete access to production databases is never granted to contractors, so it is denied. Risk level is high because the request combines a prior PII violation, multiple high-sensitivity denied requests, production PII access, and delete access. required_approvals is empty because the only approved item is R5, which needs no extra approver before activation. policy_exceptions_needed is empty because the currently denied items are blocked by role restrictions, prior violation, supervised-session routing, or non-overridable delete restriction rather than a simple approvable exception. N1 (AR-28, E-771) and N2 (AR-30, E-551) are excluded from AR-29 review.

原始 task 输入

中文翻译

审查员工 E-443 的 IT 访问申请批次 AR-29。需要根据可用资料判断：哪些权限应该批准，哪些权限应该拒绝以及拒绝原因，请求整体风险等级是多少，在任何已批准权限正式开通前还需要哪些额外审批，如果要批准当前被拒绝的项目需要哪些策略例外，批次里哪些申请不属于本次 AR-29/E-443 审查而应排除，以及应标记哪些安全风险。评估时必须综合使用角色权限矩阵、数据访问策略和访问历史。最终只能返回 JSON 对象，字段包括：approved_permissions、denied_permissions、risk_level、required_approvals、policy_exceptions_needed、excluded_requests、risk_flags。approved_permissions 和 denied_permissions 都应使用简短 snake_case 标签表示资源和权限级别；risk_level 只能是 low、medium 或 high；required_approvals 表示开通已批准权限前还需要的审批角色；policy_exceptions_needed 表示若要批准当前被拒绝项所需的例外，若没有可覆盖的例外则为空；excluded_requests 表示属于其他批次或其他员工、因此不属于本次审查的申请；risk_flags 表示识别出的安全风险。risk_flags 需要列出资料直接支持的每一个独立安全风险原因，而不是只列最高层级风险。JSON 外不要输出解释。

Original instruction

Review IT access request batch AR-29 for employee E-443 using the available sources. Determine which permissions should be approved, which should be denied and why, the overall risk level of this request, what additional approvals are required before any approved permissions can be granted, any policy exceptions that would be needed, which requests in the batch should be excluded from this review, and any security risk flags. Apply the role permission matrix, data access policy, and access history when evaluating each request. Return only a JSON object with these fields: approved_permissions, denied_permissions, risk_level, required_approvals, policy_exceptions_needed, excluded_requests, risk_flags. approved_permissions must be an array of short snake_case labels for resources and permission levels that can be granted under current policy. denied_permissions must be an array of short snake_case labels for resources and permission levels that cannot be granted. risk_level must be a short lowercase string: low, medium, or high. required_approvals must be an array of short snake_case labels for approver roles needed before any approved permissions are activated. policy_exceptions_needed must be an array of short snake_case labels for exceptions that would be required to approve any currently denied item (leave empty if no exceptions could override a denial). excluded_requests must be an array of short snake_case labels for requests in the batch that belong to other batches or other employees and are not part of AR-29. risk_flags must be an array of short snake_case labels for security risks identified. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

data_policy_ar29 data/generated/hard_v1/sources/hard_access_review_ar29/data_policy_ar29.md
# Data Access and Security Policy

## Contractor Access Restrictions

Contractors are treated as external parties and may only access systems listed
as explicitly permitted for the contractor role in the role permission matrix.
Any resource showing "none" in the matrix requires a signed Data Access Exception
(DAE) approved by the Security Lead and the department head.

## PII Access

Customer PII databases are classified as Restricted. Access requires:
1. A business justification approved by the department head
2. Security Lead sign-off
3. No prior data handling violations on record

QA and testing must use anonymized or synthetic data sets, not production PII,
unless a specific production data exception is approved by the Privacy Officer.

## Delete Permissions

Delete-level access to any production database is never granted to contractors
or analysts. This applies regardless of business justification.

## Audit Log Access

Internal audit logs are classified as Confidential. Contractor role access level
is "none" per the role matrix. Debugging access for contractors must be routed
through a supervised session with an on-staff engineer.

## Deployment Pipeline

Write access to the deployment pipeline requires the engineer role or above.
Contractor role does not include deployment write access by default.

role,resource,max_permission_level,requires_additional_approval
employee,usage_reports,read,false
employee,internal_audit_logs,read,false
employee,customer_pii_database,none,true
analyst,financial_reports,read,false
analyst,usage_reports,read,false
engineer,deployment_pipeline,write,false
engineer,usage_reports,read,false
engineer,internal_audit_logs,read,false
contractor,usage_reports,read,false
contractor,internal_audit_logs,none,true
contractor,customer_pii_database,none,true
contractor,deployment_pipeline,none,true
manager,customer_pii_database,read,true

正确解答过程

Only R5 (usage_reports read) is in the contractor role matrix as allowed, and it does not require additional approval. R1 (PII read): contractor role = none, plus prior PII handling violation makes this denied. R2 (audit logs read): contractor role = none, and contractor debugging access should use a supervised session instead of direct access, so it is denied. R3 (deployment write): contractor role = none and write access requires engineer role or above, so it is denied. R4 (PII delete): contractor role = none AND policy states delete access to production databases is never granted to contractors, so it is denied. Risk level is high because the request combines a prior PII violation, multiple high-sensitivity denied requests, production PII access, and delete access. required_approvals is empty because the only approved item is R5, which needs no extra approver before activation. policy_exceptions_needed is empty because the currently denied items are blocked by role restrictions, prior violation, supervised-session routing, or non-overridable delete restriction rather than a simple approvable exception. N1 (AR-28, E-771) and N2 (AR-30, E-551) are excluded from AR-29 review.

原始 task 输入

中文翻译

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。还需要读取 Excel side-adjustment 工作簿，计算三人最终 payout。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据、2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单，以及 final approved 的 side-adjustment 行；跨 3/31 的账单如果 source 要求 prorate，需要只纳入截止日前的金额。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买、搬出后费用、draft workbook 行、旧草稿 sheet、个人 side expense、重复 side expense 和未获三人批准的 side expense。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、side-adjustment 净调整、side adjustment 后的最终 payout、各类纳入/排除 ID 以及三个结算流程风险 boolean。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。side_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入 side adjustment 金额减去其按 allocation_rule 应承担金额；最终 payout 等于 final_refund 加 side_adjustment。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网和 side adjustment 调整之前。扣款、押金、账单和 Excel side-adjustment 选择必须输出 source 中的 item_id、row_id、bill_id 或 adjustment_id，不要自造描述性标签。三个风险字段输出 boolean。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit and utility settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, and utilities through 2026-03-31. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, and post-move-out utility periods. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, risk_flags. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, and risk_flags must be arrays of short snake_case labels. For every array field, include all and only the items directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

Allowed tools

[
  "list_sources",
  "read_source",
  "read_text_source",

  "read_pdf",
  "read_docx",
  "run_calculation",
  "lookup_vendor_status",
  "search_employee_directory",
  "check_public_calendar",
  "estimate_shipping_cost"
]

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

# HM-37 Move-out Inspection Notes

Inspection date: 2026-04-01
Unit: 4B

Room assignments:
- Alex: Bedroom A
- Bri: Bedroom B
- Chen: small room and living-room desk nook

Findings tied to final charges:
- General cleaning: kitchen surfaces, refrigerator drawers, and bathroom baseboards
  required a re-clean after keys were returned. Responsibility not assigned.
- Missing mailbox key: one mailbox key missing from returned packet. Responsibility
  unknown.
- Living room wall: two desk-anchor gouges under the window by Chen's desk nook.
  Chen confirmed the anchor strips were from his standing desk cable rail.
- Bedroom B blinds: three broken slats in bedroom B; not marked on the move-in
  checklist. Bedroom B was Bri's assigned room.

Findings not charged to tenants:
- Hall carpet was already worn and stained on the move-in checklist. The April 2
  preliminary replacement estimate was withdrawn.
- Balcony screen was bent after the March 28 wind storm. Property manager marked
  it weather-related and withdrew the charge.

Noise notes:
- A parking remote issue belongs to Unit 4C.
- A hallway scuff outside Unit 4B belongs to common-area maintenance, not HM-37.

正确解答过程

Valid deposit contributions are Alex 1200, Bri 1050, Chen 750, total 3000, giving deposit shares 40%, 35%, 25%. Final landlord deductions are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 living room wall repair is charged only to Chen, and F4 bedroom B blinds only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Base deposit refunds are Alex 1068.00, Bri 804.50, Chen 247.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and the remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. Water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. Internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds after utility settlement are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. Exclude withdrawn/preexisting/weather/other-unit/draft/voided/post-move-out/personal/unapproved lines as listed in gold.

评分明细

结果检查项ExpectedUnexpectedActual任务要求依据说明
正确landlord_deduction_total.exact880.0880.0任务要求：只计算房东最终押金扣款总额，不包括水电网、初稿估价、撤回项或其他单元项目。final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中 F1-F4 为 final_deducted，金额 285+45+420+130=880；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 也确认最终扣款为 880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确refund_pool.exact2120.02120.0任务要求：有效总押金减去房东最终扣款；这是室友间水电网调整之前的押金退款池。deposit_contributions_hm37.csv 的 valid_final 押金总额为 3000；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 确认退款支票为 2120，即 3000-880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_landlord_deduction_share.exact132.0132.0任务要求：输出 Alex 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md 要求共同/未知责任按押金比例分摊；Alex 押金占比 1200/3000=40%，只承担 F1+F2 的 40%。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_landlord_deduction_share.exact245.5245.5任务要求：输出 Bri 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Bri 押金占比 35%，承担 F1+F2 的 35%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 Bedroom B blinds 的 130。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_landlord_deduction_share.exact502.5502.5任务要求：输出 Chen 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Chen 押金占比 25%，承担 F1+F2 的 25%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 living room wall repair 的 420。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_utility_adjustment.exact47.447.4任务要求：输出 Alex 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；正数表示多拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv 和 lease rules：U1 电费扣除 Alex EV add-on 后平分，U2/U3 平分，U7 gas 按 12/16 天 prorate 后 108 平分；Alex 已支付 192.60，应承担 181.20，调整额为 11.40。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。

原始 task 输入

中文翻译

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据以及 2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买和搬出后费用。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、最终退款、有效扣款、排除项、实际应用的水电网规则和结算流程风险。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网调整之前。excluded_items 需要覆盖所有 source 中明确排除的项目；risk_flags 只列 source 直接支持的结算流程风险。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit and utility settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, and utilities through 2026-03-31. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, and post-move-out utility periods. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, risk_flags. Field definitions: landlord_deduction_total is the sum of final landlord security-deposit deductions only. refund_pool is total valid security deposit minus landlord_deduction_total before roommate utility adjustments. Each *_landlord_deduction_share is that roommate's share of landlord deductions under the housemate allocation rules. Each *_utility_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included utilities minus that roommate's responsibility for included utilities; positive means the roommate should receive more from the settlement, negative means the roommate should receive less. Each *_final_refund is that roommate's valid deposit contribution minus landlord deduction share plus utility adjustment. approved_deductions are the final landlord deduction items accepted into the settlement. excluded_items are all source rows or items explicitly excluded from the settlement across property-statement, deposit-ledger, utility, and preliminary-quote sources. utility_adjustments_applied are the included utility allocation rules actually used; use one label per included utility bill/allocation, and for the March electricity bill use one label that covers both the equal split and Alex's EV add-on rather than separate labels. risk_flags are settlement-process risks directly supported by the sources, such as single-recipient refund routing, conflicting preliminary estimates, or roommate reimbursement needs; do not use generic damage-risk labels. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, and risk_flags must be arrays of short snake_case labels. For every array field, include all and only the items directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

  unknown.

  Chen confirmed the anchor strips were from his standing desk cable rail.

  checklist. Bedroom B was Bri's assigned room.

正确解答过程

Valid deposit contributions are Alex 1200, Bri 1050, Chen 750, total 3000, giving deposit shares 40%, 35%, 25%. Final landlord deductions are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 living room wall repair is charged only to Chen, and F4 bedroom B blinds only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Base deposit refunds are Alex 1068.00, Bri 804.50, Chen 247.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and the remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. Water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. Internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds after utility settlement are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. Exclude withdrawn/preexisting/weather/other-unit/draft/voided/post-move-out/personal/unapproved lines as listed in gold.

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据以及 2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买和搬出后费用。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、最终退款、有效扣款、排除项、实际应用的水电网规则和结算流程风险。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网调整之前。excluded_items 需要覆盖所有 source 中明确排除的项目；risk_flags 只列 source 直接支持的结算流程风险。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit and utility settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, and utilities through 2026-03-31. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, and post-move-out utility periods. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, risk_flags. Field definitions: landlord_deduction_total is the sum of final landlord security-deposit deductions only. refund_pool is total valid security deposit minus landlord_deduction_total before roommate utility adjustments. Each *_landlord_deduction_share is that roommate's share of landlord deductions under the housemate allocation rules. Each *_utility_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included utilities minus that roommate's responsibility for included utilities; positive means the roommate should receive more from the settlement, negative means the roommate should receive less. Each *_final_refund is that roommate's valid deposit contribution minus landlord deduction share plus utility adjustment. approved_deductions are the final landlord deduction items accepted into the settlement. excluded_items are all source rows or items explicitly excluded from the settlement across property-statement, deposit-ledger, utility, and preliminary-quote sources. utility_adjustments_applied are the included utility allocation rules actually used; use one label per included utility bill/allocation, and for the March electricity bill use one label that covers both the equal split and Alex's EV add-on rather than separate labels. risk_flags are settlement-process risks directly supported by the sources, such as single-recipient refund routing, conflicting preliminary estimates, or roommate reimbursement needs; do not use generic damage-risk labels. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, and risk_flags must be arrays of short snake_case labels. For every array field, include all and only the items directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

Allowed tools

  "list_sources",
  "read_source",

  "read_docx",
  "run_calculation",
  "lookup_vendor_status",
  "search_employee_directory",
  "check_public_calendar",
  "estimate_shipping_cost"
]

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

# HM-37 Move-out Inspection Notes

Inspection date: 2026-04-01
Unit: 4B

Room assignments:
- Alex: Bedroom A
- Bri: Bedroom B
- Chen: small room and living-room desk nook

Findings tied to final charges:
- General cleaning: kitchen surfaces, refrigerator drawers, and bathroom baseboards
  required a re-clean after keys were returned. Responsibility not assigned.
- Missing mailbox key: one mailbox key missing from returned packet. Responsibility
  unknown.
- Living room wall: two desk-anchor gouges under the window by Chen's desk nook.
  Chen confirmed the anchor strips were from his standing desk cable rail.
- Bedroom B blinds: three broken slats in bedroom B; not marked on the move-in
  checklist. Bedroom B was Bri's assigned room.

Findings not charged to tenants:
- Hall carpet was already worn and stained on the move-in checklist. The April 2
  preliminary replacement estimate was withdrawn.
- Balcony screen was bent after the March 28 wind storm. Property manager marked
  it weather-related and withdrew the charge.

Noise notes:
- A parking remote issue belongs to Unit 4C.
- A hallway scuff outside Unit 4B belongs to common-area maintenance, not HM-37.

正确解答过程

Valid deposit contributions are Alex 1200, Bri 1050, Chen 750, total 3000, giving deposit shares 40%, 35%, 25%. Final landlord deductions are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 living room wall repair is charged only to Chen, and F4 bedroom B blinds only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Base deposit refunds are Alex 1068.00, Bri 804.50, Chen 247.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and the remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. Water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. Internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds after utility settlement are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. Exclude withdrawn/preexisting/weather/other-unit/draft/voided/post-move-out/personal/unapproved lines as listed in gold.

评分明细

结果检查项ExpectedUnexpectedActual任务要求依据说明
正确landlord_deduction_total.exact880.0880.0任务要求：只计算房东最终押金扣款总额，不包括水电网、初稿估价、撤回项或其他单元项目。final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中 F1-F4 为 final_deducted，金额 285+45+420+130=880；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 也确认最终扣款为 880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确refund_pool.exact2120.02120.0任务要求：有效总押金减去房东最终扣款；这是室友间水电网调整之前的押金退款池。deposit_contributions_hm37.csv 的 valid_final 押金总额为 3000；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 确认退款支票为 2120，即 3000-880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_landlord_deduction_share.exact132.0132.0任务要求：输出 Alex 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md 要求共同/未知责任按押金比例分摊；Alex 押金占比 1200/3000=40%，只承担 F1+F2 的 40%。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。

原始 task 输入

中文翻译

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据以及 2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买和搬出后费用。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、最终退款、有效扣款、排除项、实际应用的水电网规则和结算流程风险。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网调整之前。扣款、排除项和水电网账单选择必须输出 source 中的 item_id、row_id 或 bill_id，不要自造描述性标签。三个风险字段输出 boolean。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit and utility settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, and utilities through 2026-03-31. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, and post-move-out utility periods. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, approved_deduction_ids, excluded_property_statement_ids, valid_deposit_people, excluded_deposit_row_ids, included_utility_bill_ids, excluded_utility_bill_ids, refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, roommate_reimbursement_needed. Field definitions: landlord_deduction_total is the sum of final landlord security-deposit deductions only. refund_pool is total valid security deposit minus landlord_deduction_total before roommate utility adjustments. Each *_landlord_deduction_share is that roommate's share of landlord deductions under the housemate allocation rules. Each *_utility_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included utilities minus that roommate's responsibility for included utilities; positive means the roommate should receive more from the settlement, negative means the roommate should receive less. Each *_final_refund is that roommate's valid deposit contribution minus landlord deduction share plus utility adjustment. approved_deduction_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv for final landlord deductions accepted into the settlement. excluded_property_statement_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. valid_deposit_people must contain the person names from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv whose rows are valid_final for Unit 4B. excluded_deposit_row_ids must contain the row_id values from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. included_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv included in the roommate utility settlement. excluded_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv excluded from the roommate utility settlement. refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, and roommate_reimbursement_needed must be booleans directly determined from the sources. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. ID array fields must use exact source IDs, not free-form descriptive labels. For every array field, include all and only the source IDs directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

Final landlord deduction item_ids are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Excluded property statement item_ids are X1, X2, D1, and N1. Excluded deposit row_ids are DEP_ALEX_VOIDED_TOPUP and DEP_DANA_4C. Included utility bill_ids are U1, U2, and U3; excluded utility bill_ids are U4, U5, and U6. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 is charged only to Chen, and F4 only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: U1 electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. U2 water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. U3 internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. refund_check_to_lead_tenant=true because the refund check was issued only to Alex. preliminary_estimate_conflict=true because the preliminary quote conflicts with the final statement. roommate_reimbursement_needed=true because utility payers and responsibilities differ.

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

中文翻译

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。还需要读取 Excel side-adjustment 工作簿，计算三人最终 payout。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据、2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单，以及 final approved 的 side-adjustment 行。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买、搬出后费用、draft workbook 行、旧草稿 sheet、个人 side expense、重复 side expense 和未获三人批准的 side expense。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、side-adjustment 净调整、side adjustment 后的最终 payout、各类纳入/排除 ID 以及三个结算流程风险 boolean。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。side_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入 side adjustment 金额减去其应承担金额；最终 payout 等于 final_refund 加 side_adjustment。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网和 side adjustment 调整之前。扣款、押金、账单和 Excel side-adjustment 选择必须输出 source 中的 item_id、row_id、bill_id 或 adjustment_id，不要自造描述性标签。三个风险字段输出 boolean。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit, utility, and side-adjustment settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, utilities through 2026-03-31, and final approved side-adjustment workbook rows. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, post-move-out utility periods, draft workbook rows, old draft workbook tabs, personal side expenses, duplicate side expenses, and side expenses without all-roommate approval. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, side_adjustment_total, alex_side_adjustment, bri_side_adjustment, chen_side_adjustment, alex_final_payout, bri_final_payout, chen_final_payout, approved_deduction_ids, excluded_property_statement_ids, valid_deposit_people, excluded_deposit_row_ids, included_utility_bill_ids, excluded_utility_bill_ids, included_side_adjustment_ids, excluded_side_adjustment_ids, refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, roommate_reimbursement_needed. Field definitions: landlord_deduction_total is the sum of final landlord security-deposit deductions only. refund_pool is total valid security deposit minus landlord_deduction_total before roommate utility and side adjustments. Each *_landlord_deduction_share is that roommate's share of landlord deductions under the housemate allocation rules. Each *_utility_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included utilities minus that roommate's responsibility for included utilities; positive means the roommate should receive more from the settlement, negative means the roommate should receive less. Each *_final_refund is that roommate's valid deposit contribution minus landlord deduction share plus utility adjustment, before side adjustments. side_adjustment_total is the total amount of included final side-adjustment rows. Each *_side_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included side adjustments minus that roommate's responsibility for included side adjustments. Each *_final_payout is that roommate's final_refund plus side_adjustment. approved_deduction_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv for final landlord deductions accepted into the settlement. excluded_property_statement_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. valid_deposit_people must contain the person names from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv whose rows are valid_final for Unit 4B. excluded_deposit_row_ids must contain the row_id values from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. included_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv included in the roommate utility settlement. excluded_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv excluded from the roommate utility settlement. included_side_adjustment_ids must contain adjustment_id values from side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx included in the roommate side-adjustment settlement. excluded_side_adjustment_ids must contain adjustment_id values from side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx excluded from the roommate side-adjustment settlement. refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, and roommate_reimbursement_needed must be booleans directly determined from the sources. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. ID array fields must use exact source IDs, not free-form descriptive labels. For every array field, include all and only the source IDs directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

# HM-37 Move-out Inspection Notes

Inspection date: 2026-04-01
Unit: 4B

Room assignments:
- Alex: Bedroom A
- Bri: Bedroom B
- Chen: small room and living-room desk nook

Findings tied to final charges:
- General cleaning: kitchen surfaces, refrigerator drawers, and bathroom baseboards
  required a re-clean after keys were returned. Responsibility not assigned.
- Missing mailbox key: one mailbox key missing from returned packet. Responsibility
  unknown.
- Living room wall: two desk-anchor gouges under the window by Chen's desk nook.
  Chen confirmed the anchor strips were from his standing desk cable rail.
- Bedroom B blinds: three broken slats in bedroom B; not marked on the move-in
  checklist. Bedroom B was Bri's assigned room.

Findings not charged to tenants:
- Hall carpet was already worn and stained on the move-in checklist. The April 2
  preliminary replacement estimate was withdrawn.
- Balcony screen was bent after the March 28 wind storm. Property manager marked
  it weather-related and withdrew the charge.

Noise notes:
- A parking remote issue belongs to Unit 4C.
- A hallway scuff outside Unit 4B belongs to common-area maintenance, not HM-37.

正确解答过程

Valid deposit rows are DEP_ALEX_VALID, DEP_BRI_VALID, and DEP_CHEN_VALID for Alex 1200, Bri 1050, Chen 750, total 3000, giving deposit shares 40%, 35%, 25%. Final landlord deduction item_ids are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Excluded property statement item_ids are X1, X2, D1, and N1. Excluded deposit row_ids are DEP_ALEX_VOIDED_TOPUP and DEP_DANA_4C. Included utility bill_ids are U1, U2, and U3; excluded utility bill_ids are U4, U5, and U6. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 is charged only to Chen, and F4 only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: U1 electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. U2 water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. U3 internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds before side adjustments are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. Included side adjustment IDs are SA1 18 paid by Alex, SA2 90 paid by Bri, and SA3 6 paid by Alex, total 114. Each roommate is responsible for 38.00. Paid totals are Alex 24, Bri 90, Chen 0, so side adjustments are Alex -14.00, Bri +52.00, Chen -38.00. Final payouts after side adjustments are Alex 1101.40, Bri 822.30, Chen 196.30. Excluded side adjustment IDs are SA4, SA5, SA6, SA7, SA8, SA9, OLD1, OLD2, and OLD3. refund_check_to_lead_tenant=true because the refund check was issued only to Alex. preliminary_estimate_conflict=true because the preliminary quote conflicts with the final statement. roommate_reimbursement_needed=true because utility and side-adjustment payers and responsibilities differ.

中文翻译

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。还需要读取 Excel side-adjustment 工作簿，计算三人最终 payout。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据、2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单，以及 final approved 的 side-adjustment 行；跨 3/31 的账单如果 source 要求 prorate，需要只纳入截止日前的金额。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买、搬出后费用、draft workbook 行、旧草稿 sheet、个人 side expense、重复 side expense 和未获三人批准的 side expense。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、side-adjustment 净调整、side adjustment 后的最终 payout、各类纳入/排除 ID 以及三个结算流程风险 boolean。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。side_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入 side adjustment 金额减去其按 allocation_rule 应承担金额；最终 payout 等于 final_refund 加 side_adjustment。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网和 side adjustment 调整之前。扣款、押金、账单和 Excel side-adjustment 选择必须输出 source 中的 item_id、row_id、bill_id 或 adjustment_id，不要自造描述性标签。三个风险字段输出 boolean。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit, utility, and side-adjustment settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, utilities through 2026-03-31 including utility rows that must be prorated through that date, and final approved side-adjustment workbook rows. For utility rows spanning the cutoff, prorate only when the source row/rules say to prorate; for side adjustments, apply each included row's allocation_rule. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, post-move-out utility periods, draft workbook rows, old draft workbook tabs, personal side expenses, duplicate side expenses, and side expenses without all-roommate approval. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, side_adjustment_total, alex_side_adjustment, bri_side_adjustment, chen_side_adjustment, alex_final_payout, bri_final_payout, chen_final_payout, approved_deduction_ids, excluded_property_statement_ids, valid_deposit_people, excluded_deposit_row_ids, included_utility_bill_ids, excluded_utility_bill_ids, included_side_adjustment_ids, excluded_side_adjustment_ids, refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, roommate_reimbursement_needed. Field definitions: landlord_deduction_total is the sum of final landlord security-deposit deductions only. refund_pool is total valid security deposit minus landlord_deduction_total before roommate utility and side adjustments. Each *_landlord_deduction_share is that roommate's share of landlord deductions under the housemate allocation rules. Each *_utility_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for the included portion of utilities minus that roommate's responsibility for included utilities; positive means the roommate should receive more from the settlement, negative means the roommate should receive less. Each *_final_refund is that roommate's valid deposit contribution minus landlord deduction share plus utility adjustment, before side adjustments. side_adjustment_total is the total amount of included final side-adjustment rows. Each *_side_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included side adjustments minus that roommate's responsibility under each included side-adjustment allocation_rule. Each *_final_payout is that roommate's final_refund plus side_adjustment. approved_deduction_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv for final landlord deductions accepted into the settlement. excluded_property_statement_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. valid_deposit_people must contain the person names from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv whose rows are valid_final for Unit 4B. excluded_deposit_row_ids must contain the row_id values from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. included_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv included in the roommate utility settlement. excluded_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv excluded from the roommate utility settlement. included_side_adjustment_ids must contain adjustment_id values from side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx included in the roommate side-adjustment settlement. excluded_side_adjustment_ids must contain adjustment_id values from side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx excluded from the roommate side-adjustment settlement. refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, and roommate_reimbursement_needed must be booleans directly determined from the sources. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. ID array fields must use exact source IDs, not free-form descriptive labels. For every array field, include all and only the source IDs directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

If a final utility bill spans both before and after 2026-03-31, include only
the portion through 2026-03-31 when the source row says to prorate. Count
service days inclusively. For a row marked
`prorate_through_2026_03_31_equal_split`, calculate:

1. total inclusive service days,
2. inclusive service days through 2026-03-31,
3. `total_amount * included_days / total_days`,
4. then split that included amount equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

Side adjustments are handled after landlord deductions and utility settlement.
Use only the `final_side_adjustments` sheet in the side-adjustment workbook.
Include rows only when `status=final` and `include_in_settlement=yes`.
For `allocation_rule=equal_split`, split the included amount equally among Alex,
Bri, and Chen. For `allocation_rule=deposit_share`, allocate the responsibility
by the original valid deposit shares. Exclude old draft sheets, draft rows,
voided rows, other-unit rows, personal or personal-subset expenses, duplicates
of landlord deductions, and any side expense without all-roommate approval.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

正确解答过程

Valid deposit rows are DEP_ALEX_VALID, DEP_BRI_VALID, and DEP_CHEN_VALID for Alex 1200, Bri 1050, Chen 750, total 3000, giving deposit shares 40%, 35%, 25%. Final landlord deduction item_ids are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Excluded property statement item_ids are X1, X2, D1, and N1. Excluded deposit row_ids are DEP_ALEX_VOIDED_TOPUP and DEP_DANA_4C. Included utility bill_ids are U1, U2, U3, and U7; excluded utility bill_ids are U4, U5, U6, U8, and U9. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 is charged only to Chen, and F4 only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: U1 electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. U2 water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. U3 internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. U7 gas spans 2026-03-20 to 2026-04-04, 16 inclusive days; include 12 days through 2026-03-31, so 144*(12/16)=108, split equally at 36 each and treated as Chen paid 108 for settlement. Utility responsibilities are Alex 181.20, Bri 145.20, Chen 145.20; included paid amounts are Alex 192.60, Bri 75.00, Chen 204.00, so utility net adjustments are Alex +11.40, Bri -70.20, Chen +58.80. Final refunds before side adjustments are Alex 1079.40, Bri 734.30, Chen 306.30. Included side adjustment IDs are SA1 18 paid by Alex, SA2 90 paid by Bri, SA3 6 paid by Alex, and SA10 75 paid by Chen, total 189. SA1-SA3 are equal split: 114/3=38 each. SA10 is deposit_share: Alex 30.00, Bri 26.25, Chen 18.75. Side responsibilities are Alex 68.00, Bri 64.25, Chen 56.75; paid totals are Alex 24.00, Bri 90.00, Chen 75.00, so side adjustments are Alex -44.00, Bri +25.75, Chen +18.25. Final payouts after side adjustments are Alex 1035.40, Bri 760.05, Chen 324.55. Excluded side adjustment IDs are SA4, SA5, SA6, SA7, SA8, SA9, SA11, SA12, OLD1, OLD2, and OLD3. refund_check_to_lead_tenant=true because the refund check was issued only to Alex. preliminary_estimate_conflict=true because the preliminary quote conflicts with the final statement. roommate_reimbursement_needed=true because utility and side-adjustment payers and responsibilities differ.

2. read_text_source {'source_id': 'lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37'}
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

If a final utility bill spans both before and after 2026-03-31, include only
the portion through 2026-03-31 when the source row says to prorate. Count
service days inclusively. For a row marked
`prorate_through_2026_03_31_equal_split`, calculate:

1. total inclusive service days,
2. inclusive service days through 2026-03-31,
3. `total_amount * included_days / total_days`,
4. then split that included amount equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

Side adjustments are handled after landlord deductions and utility settlement.
Use only the `final_side_adjustments` sheet in the side-adjustment workbook.
Include rows only when `status=final` and `include_in_settlement=yes`.
For `allocation_rule=equal_split`, split the included amount equally among Alex,
Bri, and Chen. For `allocation_rule=deposit_share`, allocate the responsibility
by the original valid deposit shares. Exclude old draft sheets, draft rows,
voided rows, other-unit rows, personal or personal-subset expenses, duplicates
of landlord deductions, and any side expense without all-roommate approval.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

评分明细

结果检查项ExpectedUnexpectedActual任务要求依据说明
正确landlord_deduction_total.exact880.0880.0任务要求：只计算房东最终押金扣款总额，不包括水电网、初稿估价、撤回项或其他单元项目。final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中 F1-F4 为 final_deducted，金额 285+45+420+130=880；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 也确认最终扣款为 880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确refund_pool.exact2120.02120.0任务要求：有效总押金减去房东最终扣款；这是室友间水电网调整之前的押金退款池。deposit_contributions_hm37.csv 的 valid_final 押金总额为 3000；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 确认退款支票为 2120，即 3000-880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_landlord_deduction_share.exact132.0132.0任务要求：输出 Alex 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md 要求共同/未知责任按押金比例分摊；Alex 押金占比 1200/3000=40%，只承担 F1+F2 的 40%。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_landlord_deduction_share.exact245.5245.5任务要求：输出 Bri 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Bri 押金占比 35%，承担 F1+F2 的 35%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 Bedroom B blinds 的 130。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_landlord_deduction_share.exact502.5502.5任务要求：输出 Chen 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Chen 押金占比 25%，承担 F1+F2 的 25%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 living room wall repair 的 420。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_utility_adjustment.exact11.411.4任务要求：输出 Alex 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；正数表示多拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv 和 lease rules：U1 电费扣除 Alex EV add-on 后平分，U2/U3 平分，U7 gas 按 12/16 天 prorate 后 108 平分；Alex 已支付 192.60，应承担 181.20，调整额为 11.40。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_utility_adjustment.exact-70.2-70.2任务要求：输出 Bri 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；负数表示少拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv：Bri 支付网络 75，应承担 U1/U2/U3/U7 合计 145.20，所以调整额为 75-145.20=-70.20。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_utility_adjustment.exact58.858.8任务要求：输出 Chen 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；负数表示少拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv：Chen 支付水费/垃圾费 96，并对 U7 只计入 prorated 108，纳入支付合计 204，应承担 145.20，所以调整额为 58.80。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_final_refund.exact1079.41079.4任务要求：输出 Alex 的有效押金出资减去房东扣款份额，再加水电网调整额后的最终退款。Alex 押金基数为 1200-132=1068，再加 utility adjustment 11.40，最终 1079.40。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_final_refund.exact734.3734.3任务要求：输出 Bri 的有效押金出资减去房东扣款份额，再加水电网调整额后的最终退款。Bri 押金基数为 1050-245.50=804.50，再加 utility adjustment -70.20，最终 734.30。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_final_refund.exact306.3306.3任务要求：输出 Chen 的有效押金出资减去房东扣款份额，再加水电网调整额后的最终退款。Chen 押金基数为 750-502.50=247.50，再加 utility adjustment 58.80，最终 306.30。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确side_adjustment_total.exact189.0189.0任务要求：输出纳入结算的 final approved side-adjustment 行金额总和。side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx 的 final_side_adjustments sheet 中 SA1、SA2、SA3、SA10 纳入结算，总额 18+90+6+75=189。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_side_adjustment.exact-44.0-44.0任务要求：输出 Alex 的 side-adjustment 净调整额，即已支付纳入 side adjustment 金额减去自己应承担金额。side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx：Alex 支付 SA1 18 和 SA3 6，共 24；SA1-SA3 equal split 责任 38，SA10 deposit_share 责任 30，总责任 68，所以 Alex 调整额为 24-68=-44。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_side_adjustment.exact25.7525.75任务要求：输出 Bri 的 side-adjustment 净调整额，即已支付纳入 side adjustment 金额减去自己应承担金额。side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx：Bri 支付 SA2 90；SA1-SA3 equal split 责任 38，SA10 deposit_share 责任 26.25，总责任 64.25，所以 Bri 调整额为 25.75。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_side_adjustment.exact18.2518.25任务要求：输出 Chen 的 side-adjustment 净调整额，即已支付纳入 side adjustment 金额减去自己应承担金额。side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx：Chen 支付 SA10 75；SA1-SA3 equal split 责任 38，SA10 deposit_share 责任 18.75，总责任 56.75，所以 Chen 调整额为 18.25。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。还需要读取 Excel side-adjustment 工作簿，计算三人最终 payout。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据、2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单，以及 final approved 的 side-adjustment 行；跨 3/31 的账单如果 source 要求 prorate，需要只纳入截止日前的金额。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买、搬出后费用、draft workbook 行、旧草稿 sheet、个人 side expense、重复 side expense 和未获三人批准的 side expense。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、side-adjustment 净调整、side adjustment 后的最终 payout、各类纳入/排除 ID 以及三个结算流程风险 boolean。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。side_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入 side adjustment 金额减去其按 allocation_rule 应承担金额；最终 payout 等于 final_refund 加 side_adjustment。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网和 side adjustment 调整之前。扣款、押金、账单和 Excel side-adjustment 选择必须输出 source 中的 item_id、row_id、bill_id 或 adjustment_id，不要自造描述性标签。三个风险字段输出 boolean。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit and utility settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, and utilities through 2026-03-31. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, and post-move-out utility periods. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, risk_flags. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, and risk_flags must be arrays of short snake_case labels. For every array field, include all and only the items directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

# HM-37 Move-out Inspection Notes

Inspection date: 2026-04-01
Unit: 4B

Room assignments:
- Alex: Bedroom A
- Bri: Bedroom B
- Chen: small room and living-room desk nook

Findings tied to final charges:
- General cleaning: kitchen surfaces, refrigerator drawers, and bathroom baseboards
  required a re-clean after keys were returned. Responsibility not assigned.
- Missing mailbox key: one mailbox key missing from returned packet. Responsibility
  unknown.
- Living room wall: two desk-anchor gouges under the window by Chen's desk nook.
  Chen confirmed the anchor strips were from his standing desk cable rail.
- Bedroom B blinds: three broken slats in bedroom B; not marked on the move-in
  checklist. Bedroom B was Bri's assigned room.

Findings not charged to tenants:
- Hall carpet was already worn and stained on the move-in checklist. The April 2
  preliminary replacement estimate was withdrawn.
- Balcony screen was bent after the March 28 wind storm. Property manager marked
  it weather-related and withdrew the charge.

Noise notes:
- A parking remote issue belongs to Unit 4C.
- A hallway scuff outside Unit 4B belongs to common-area maintenance, not HM-37.

正确解答过程

Valid deposit contributions are Alex 1200, Bri 1050, Chen 750, total 3000, giving deposit shares 40%, 35%, 25%. Final landlord deductions are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 living room wall repair is charged only to Chen, and F4 bedroom B blinds only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Base deposit refunds are Alex 1068.00, Bri 804.50, Chen 247.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and the remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. Water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. Internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds after utility settlement are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. Exclude withdrawn/preexisting/weather/other-unit/draft/voided/post-move-out/personal/unapproved lines as listed in gold.

2. read_text_source {'source_id': 'lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37'}
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

原始 task 输入

中文翻译

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据以及 2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买和搬出后费用。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、最终退款、有效扣款、排除项、实际应用的水电网规则和结算流程风险。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网调整之前。excluded_items 需要覆盖所有 source 中明确排除的项目；risk_flags 只列 source 直接支持的结算流程风险。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit and utility settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, and utilities through 2026-03-31. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, and post-move-out utility periods. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, risk_flags. Field definitions: landlord_deduction_total is the sum of final landlord security-deposit deductions only. refund_pool is total valid security deposit minus landlord_deduction_total before roommate utility adjustments. Each *_landlord_deduction_share is that roommate's share of landlord deductions under the housemate allocation rules. Each *_utility_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included utilities minus that roommate's responsibility for included utilities; positive means the roommate should receive more from the settlement, negative means the roommate should receive less. Each *_final_refund is that roommate's valid deposit contribution minus landlord deduction share plus utility adjustment. approved_deductions are the final landlord deduction items accepted into the settlement. excluded_items are all source rows or items explicitly excluded from the settlement across property-statement, deposit-ledger, utility, and preliminary-quote sources. utility_adjustments_applied are the included utility allocation rules actually used; use one label per included utility bill/allocation, and for the March electricity bill use one label that covers both the equal split and Alex's EV add-on rather than separate labels. risk_flags are settlement-process risks directly supported by the sources, such as single-recipient refund routing, conflicting preliminary estimates, or roommate reimbursement needs; do not use generic damage-risk labels. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. approved_deductions, excluded_items, utility_adjustments_applied, and risk_flags must be arrays of short snake_case labels. For every array field, include all and only the items directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.
