lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

  unknown.
- Living room wall: two desk-anchor gouges under the window by Chen's desk nook.
  Chen confirmed the anchor strips were from his standing desk cable rail.
- Bedroom B blinds: three broken slats in bedroom B; not marked on the move-in
  checklist. Bedroom B was Bri's assigned room.

Findings not charged to tenants:
- Hall carpet was already worn and stained on the move-in checklist. The April 2
  preliminary replacement estimate was withdrawn.
- Balcony screen was bent after the March 28 wind storm. Property manager marked
  it weather-related and withdrew the charge.

- A parking remote issue belongs to Unit 4C.

Final landlord deductions are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 living room wall repair is charged only to Chen, and F4 bedroom B blinds only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Base deposit refunds are Alex 1068.00, Bri 804.50, Chen 247.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and the remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. Water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. Internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds after utility settlement are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. Exclude withdrawn/preexisting/weather/other-unit/draft/voided/post-move-out/personal/unapproved lines as listed in gold.

评分明细

结果检查项ExpectedUnexpectedActual任务要求依据说明
正确landlord_deduction_total.exact880.0880.0任务要求：只计算房东最终押金扣款总额，不包括水电网、初稿估价、撤回项或其他单元项目。final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中 F1-F4 为 final_deducted，金额 285+45+420+130=880；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 也确认最终扣款为 880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确refund_pool.exact2120.02120.0任务要求：有效总押金减去房东最终扣款；这是室友间水电网调整之前的押金退款池。deposit_contributions_hm37.csv 的 valid_final 押金总额为 3000；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 确认退款支票为 2120，即 3000-880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_landlord_deduction_share.exact132.0132.0任务要求：输出 Alex 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md 要求共同/未知责任按押金比例分摊；Alex 押金占比 1200/3000=40%，只承担 F1+F2 的 40%。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_landlord_deduction_share.exact245.5245.5任务要求：输出 Bri 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Bri 押金占比 35%，承担 F1+F2 的 35%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 Bedroom B blinds 的 130。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_landlord_deduction_share.exact502.5502.5任务要求：输出 Chen 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Chen 押金占比 25%，承担 F1+F2 的 25%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 living room wall repair 的 420。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_utility_adjustment.exact47.447.4任务要求：输出 Alex 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；正数表示多拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv 和 lease rules：U1 电费扣除 Alex EV add-on 后平分，U2/U3 平分，U7 gas 按 12/16 天 prorate 后 108 平分；Alex 已支付 192.60，应承担 181.20，调整额为 11.40。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。

原始 task 输入

中文翻译

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据以及 2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买和搬出后费用。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、最终退款、有效扣款、排除项、实际应用的水电网规则和结算流程风险。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网调整之前。扣款、排除项和水电网账单选择必须输出 source 中的 item_id、row_id 或 bill_id，不要自造描述性标签。三个风险字段输出 boolean。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit and utility settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, and utilities through 2026-03-31. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, and post-move-out utility periods. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, approved_deduction_ids, excluded_property_statement_ids, valid_deposit_people, excluded_deposit_row_ids, included_utility_bill_ids, excluded_utility_bill_ids, refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, roommate_reimbursement_needed. Field definitions: landlord_deduction_total is the sum of final landlord security-deposit deductions only. refund_pool is total valid security deposit minus landlord_deduction_total before roommate utility adjustments. Each *_landlord_deduction_share is that roommate's share of landlord deductions under the housemate allocation rules. Each *_utility_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included utilities minus that roommate's responsibility for included utilities; positive means the roommate should receive more from the settlement, negative means the roommate should receive less. Each *_final_refund is that roommate's valid deposit contribution minus landlord deduction share plus utility adjustment. approved_deduction_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv for final landlord deductions accepted into the settlement. excluded_property_statement_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. valid_deposit_people must contain the person names from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv whose rows are valid_final for Unit 4B. excluded_deposit_row_ids must contain the row_id values from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. included_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv included in the roommate utility settlement. excluded_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv excluded from the roommate utility settlement. refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, and roommate_reimbursement_needed must be booleans directly determined from the sources. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. ID array fields must use exact source IDs, not free-form descriptive labels. For every array field, include all and only the source IDs directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

Final landlord deduction item_ids are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Excluded property statement item_ids are X1, X2, D1, and N1. Excluded deposit row_ids are DEP_ALEX_VOIDED_TOPUP and DEP_DANA_4C. Included utility bill_ids are U1, U2, and U3; excluded utility bill_ids are U4, U5, and U6. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 is charged only to Chen, and F4 only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: U1 electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. U2 water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. U3 internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. refund_check_to_lead_tenant=true because the refund check was issued only to Alex. preliminary_estimate_conflict=true because the preliminary quote conflicts with the final statement. roommate_reimbursement_needed=true because utility payers and responsibilities differ.

评分明细

结果检查项ExpectedUnexpectedActual任务要求依据说明
正确landlord_deduction_total.exact880.0880.0任务要求：只计算房东最终押金扣款总额，不包括水电网、初稿估价、撤回项或其他单元项目。final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中 F1-F4 为 final_deducted，金额 285+45+420+130=880；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 也确认最终扣款为 880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确refund_pool.exact2120.02120.0任务要求：有效总押金减去房东最终扣款；这是室友间水电网调整之前的押金退款池。deposit_contributions_hm37.csv 的 valid_final 押金总额为 3000；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 确认退款支票为 2120，即 3000-880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_landlord_deduction_share.exact132.0132.0任务要求：输出 Alex 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md 要求共同/未知责任按押金比例分摊；Alex 押金占比 1200/3000=40%，只承担 F1+F2 的 40%。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_landlord_deduction_share.exact245.5245.5任务要求：输出 Bri 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Bri 押金占比 35%，承担 F1+F2 的 35%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 Bedroom B blinds 的 130。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_landlord_deduction_share.exact502.5502.5任务要求：输出 Chen 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Chen 押金占比 25%，承担 F1+F2 的 25%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 living room wall repair 的 420。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_utility_adjustment.exact47.447.4任务要求：输出 Alex 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；正数表示多拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv 和 lease rules：U1 电费扣除 Alex EV add-on 后平分，U2/U3 平分，U7 gas 按 12/16 天 prorate 后 108 平分；Alex 已支付 192.60，应承担 181.20，调整额为 11.40。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_utility_adjustment.exact-34.2-34.2任务要求：输出 Bri 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；负数表示少拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv：Bri 支付网络 75，应承担 U1/U2/U3/U7 合计 145.20，所以调整额为 75-145.20=-70.20。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_utility_adjustment.exact-13.2-13.2任务要求：输出 Chen 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；负数表示少拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv：Chen 支付水费/垃圾费 96，并对 U7 只计入 prorated 108，纳入支付合计 204，应承担 145.20，所以调整额为 58.80。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确alex_final_refund.exact1115.41115.4任务要求：输出 Alex 的有效押金出资减去房东扣款份额，再加水电网调整额后的最终退款。Alex 押金基数为 1200-132=1068，再加 utility adjustment 11.40，最终 1079.40。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_final_refund.exact770.3770.3任务要求：输出 Bri 的有效押金出资减去房东扣款份额，再加水电网调整额后的最终退款。Bri 押金基数为 1050-245.50=804.50，再加 utility adjustment -70.20，最终 734.30。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_final_refund.exact234.3234.3任务要求：输出 Chen 的有效押金出资减去房东扣款份额，再加水电网调整额后的最终退款。Chen 押金基数为 750-502.50=247.50，再加 utility adjustment 58.80，最终 306.30。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
错误refund_check_to_lead_tenant.exactTrueFalse任务要求：如果房东退款支票只发给 lead tenant，则输出 true，否则 false。lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 说明 2120 退款支票只发给 lead tenant Alex。错误：根据 source 应为 True，但模型输出为 False。lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 说明 2120 退款支票只发给 lead tenant Alex。
错误preliminary_estimate_conflict.exactTrueFalse任务要求：如果初稿报价和最终账单存在冲突或替代关系，则输出 true，否则 false。preliminary_cleaning_quote_hm37.txt 标注初稿报价；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 说明 April 2 preliminary estimate 不再有效。错误：根据 source 应为 True，但模型输出为 False。preliminary_cleaning_quote_hm37.txt 标注初稿报价；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 说明 April 2 preliminary estimate 不再有效。
错误roommate_reimbursement_needed.exactTrueFalse任务要求：如果水电网或 side adjustment 由不同室友代付且需要室友间二次调整，则输出 true，否则 false。final_utilities_hm37.csv 和 side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx 显示代付人与责任人不完全一致，需要室友间二次调整。错误：根据 source 应为 True，但模型输出为 False。final_utilities_hm37.csv 和 side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx 显示代付人与责任人不完全一致，需要室友间二次调整。
正确approved_deduction_ids.contains.f1f1['f1', 'f2', 'f3', 'f4']任务要求：输出 final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中纳入结算的最终房东扣款 item_id。final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中 F1 为 Unit 4B 的 final_deducted 清洁返工扣款。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确approved_deduction_ids.contains.f2f2['f1', 'f2', 'f3', 'f4']任务要求：输出 final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中纳入结算的最终房东扣款 item_id。final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中 F2 为 Unit 4B 的 final_deducted 邮箱钥匙替换扣款。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。

原始 task 输入

中文翻译

审查 HM-37 合租退租结算，根据可用资料计算 4B 单元 Alex、Bri、Chen 的最终押金和水电网结算。还需要读取 Excel side-adjustment 工作簿，计算三人最终 payout。只能使用最终合租规则、最终房东账单、有效押金 ledger、检查证据、2026-03-31 及以前的水电网账单，以及 final approved 的 side-adjustment 行。需要排除初稿估价、撤回扣款、其他单元记录、voided 押金、搬入前已有损坏、天气损坏、个人订阅、未批准购买、搬出后费用、draft workbook 行、旧草稿 sheet、个人 side expense、重复 side expense 和未获三人批准的 side expense。输出字段包括房东最终扣款总额、退款池、三个人的房东扣款份额、水电网净调整、side-adjustment 净调整、side adjustment 后的最终 payout、各类纳入/排除 ID 以及三个结算流程风险 boolean。utility_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入结算水电网金额减去其应承担金额；正数表示应多拿回，负数表示应少拿回。side_adjustment 表示该室友已支付的纳入 side adjustment 金额减去其应承担金额；最终 payout 等于 final_refund 加 side_adjustment。refund_pool 是有效总押金减房东最终扣款，发生在室友间水电网和 side adjustment 调整之前。扣款、押金、账单和 Excel side-adjustment 选择必须输出 source 中的 item_id、row_id、bill_id 或 adjustment_id，不要自造描述性标签。三个风险字段输出 boolean。

Original instruction

Review the household move-out settlement HM-37 using the available sources. Calculate the final security-deposit, utility, and side-adjustment settlement for Unit 4B roommates Alex, Bri, and Chen. Use only the final lease/housemate rules, final property statement, valid deposit ledger rows, inspection evidence, utilities through 2026-03-31, and final approved side-adjustment workbook rows. Exclude preliminary estimates, withdrawn charges, other-unit rows, voided deposit rows, preexisting damage, weather-related damage, personal subscriptions, unapproved purchases, post-move-out utility periods, draft workbook rows, old draft workbook tabs, personal side expenses, duplicate side expenses, and side expenses without all-roommate approval. Return only a JSON object with these fields: landlord_deduction_total, refund_pool, alex_landlord_deduction_share, bri_landlord_deduction_share, chen_landlord_deduction_share, alex_utility_adjustment, bri_utility_adjustment, chen_utility_adjustment, alex_final_refund, bri_final_refund, chen_final_refund, side_adjustment_total, alex_side_adjustment, bri_side_adjustment, chen_side_adjustment, alex_final_payout, bri_final_payout, chen_final_payout, approved_deduction_ids, excluded_property_statement_ids, valid_deposit_people, excluded_deposit_row_ids, included_utility_bill_ids, excluded_utility_bill_ids, included_side_adjustment_ids, excluded_side_adjustment_ids, refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, roommate_reimbursement_needed. Field definitions: landlord_deduction_total is the sum of final landlord security-deposit deductions only. refund_pool is total valid security deposit minus landlord_deduction_total before roommate utility and side adjustments. Each *_landlord_deduction_share is that roommate's share of landlord deductions under the housemate allocation rules. Each *_utility_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included utilities minus that roommate's responsibility for included utilities; positive means the roommate should receive more from the settlement, negative means the roommate should receive less. Each *_final_refund is that roommate's valid deposit contribution minus landlord deduction share plus utility adjustment, before side adjustments. side_adjustment_total is the total amount of included final side-adjustment rows. Each *_side_adjustment is amount paid by that roommate for included side adjustments minus that roommate's responsibility for included side adjustments. Each *_final_payout is that roommate's final_refund plus side_adjustment. approved_deduction_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv for final landlord deductions accepted into the settlement. excluded_property_statement_ids must contain the item_id values from final_property_statement_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. valid_deposit_people must contain the person names from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv whose rows are valid_final for Unit 4B. excluded_deposit_row_ids must contain the row_id values from deposit_contributions_hm37.csv that are excluded from the settlement. included_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv included in the roommate utility settlement. excluded_utility_bill_ids must contain bill_id values from final_utilities_hm37.csv excluded from the roommate utility settlement. included_side_adjustment_ids must contain adjustment_id values from side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx included in the roommate side-adjustment settlement. excluded_side_adjustment_ids must contain adjustment_id values from side_adjustment_workbook_hm37.xlsx excluded from the roommate side-adjustment settlement. refund_check_to_lead_tenant, preliminary_estimate_conflict, and roommate_reimbursement_needed must be booleans directly determined from the sources. Numeric fields must be dollar amounts rounded to two decimals. ID array fields must use exact source IDs, not free-form descriptive labels. For every array field, include all and only the source IDs directly supported by the sources; do not add speculative, generic, duplicate, or unsupported labels. Do not include explanation outside the JSON.

lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37 data/generated/hard_v2/sources/hard_household_moveout_deposit_hm37/lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

正确解答过程

Valid deposit rows are DEP_ALEX_VALID, DEP_BRI_VALID, and DEP_CHEN_VALID for Alex 1200, Bri 1050, Chen 750, total 3000, giving deposit shares 40%, 35%, 25%. Final landlord deduction item_ids are F1 285, F2 45, F3 420, and F4 130, total 880; refund pool is 3000-880=2120. Excluded property statement item_ids are X1, X2, D1, and N1. Excluded deposit row_ids are DEP_ALEX_VOIDED_TOPUP and DEP_DANA_4C. Included utility bill_ids are U1, U2, and U3; excluded utility bill_ids are U4, U5, and U6. Shared/unknown deductions are F1+F2=330, allocated by deposit share: Alex 132, Bri 115.50, Chen 82.50. F3 is charged only to Chen, and F4 only to Bri, so landlord deduction shares are Alex 132.00, Bri 245.50, Chen 502.50. Utilities through 2026-03-31: U1 electricity 192.60 paid by Alex, with 36.00 EV add-on to Alex and remaining 156.60 split equally, so Alex's electric responsibility is 88.20 and Bri/Chen each 52.20. U2 water/trash 96 paid by Chen is split equally at 32 each. U3 internet 75 paid by Bri is split equally at 25 each. Utility net adjustments are Alex +47.40, Bri -34.20, Chen -13.20. Final refunds before side adjustments are Alex 1115.40, Bri 770.30, Chen 234.30. Included side adjustment IDs are SA1 18 paid by Alex, SA2 90 paid by Bri, and SA3 6 paid by Alex, total 114. Each roommate is responsible for 38.00. Paid totals are Alex 24, Bri 90, Chen 0, so side adjustments are Alex -14.00, Bri +52.00, Chen -38.00. Final payouts after side adjustments are Alex 1101.40, Bri 822.30, Chen 196.30. Excluded side adjustment IDs are SA4, SA5, SA6, SA7, SA8, SA9, OLD1, OLD2, and OLD3. refund_check_to_lead_tenant=true because the refund check was issued only to Alex. preliminary_estimate_conflict=true because the preliminary quote conflicts with the final statement. roommate_reimbursement_needed=true because utility and side-adjustment payers and responsibilities differ.

2. read_text_source {'source_id': 'lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37'}
# HM-37 Lease and Housemate Settlement Rules

## Current rule set for Unit 4B, lease ending 2026-03-31

Security deposit held by the landlord belongs to the roommates in the same
amounts originally contributed by each person. For HM-37, use the deposit
ledger marked `valid_final`.

Landlord deductions that are confirmed on the final property statement should
be allocated as follows:

1. Shared or unknown-responsibility deductions are allocated by original deposit
   share.
2. Deductions tied to an identified roommate's assigned room or documented
   action are charged to that roommate only.
3. Withdrawn, draft, prior estimate, other-unit, move-in-preexisting, and
   weather-damage lines are excluded.
4. Normal wear and tear is not deductible.

Utilities are not landlord deductions unless the final property statement says
otherwise. For the roommate settlement, utilities through 2026-03-31 are split
equally among Alex, Bri, and Chen after applying any bill-specific rider.

Bill-specific rider for the March 2026 electricity bill:
- The EV charging add-on is Alex's personal charge.
- The remaining electricity amount is split equally.

Internet for March 2026 is a shared household utility. Streaming services,
replacement cables, and post-move-out service periods are personal or excluded
unless all three roommates approved them in writing.

## Obsolete clause, do not use

The 2024 draft housemate agreement split all shared deductions equally. That
draft was replaced by the current rule set above after Bri moved into bedroom B.
Do not use the 2024 equal-deposit split rule for HM-37.

评分明细

结果检查项ExpectedUnexpectedActual任务要求依据说明
正确landlord_deduction_total.exact880.0880.0任务要求：只计算房东最终押金扣款总额，不包括水电网、初稿估价、撤回项或其他单元项目。final_property_statement_hm37.csv 中 F1-F4 为 final_deducted，金额 285+45+420+130=880；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 也确认最终扣款为 880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确refund_pool.exact2120.02120.0任务要求：有效总押金减去房东最终扣款；这是室友间水电网调整之前的押金退款池。deposit_contributions_hm37.csv 的 valid_final 押金总额为 3000；lead_tenant_email_hm37.txt 确认退款支票为 2120，即 3000-880。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
错误alex_landlord_deduction_share.exact132.0110.0任务要求：输出 Alex 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md 要求共同/未知责任按押金比例分摊；Alex 押金占比 1200/3000=40%，只承担 F1+F2 的 40%。错误：根据 source 应为 132.0，但模型输出为 110.0。lease_and_housemate_rules_hm37.md 要求共同/未知责任按押金比例分摊；Alex 押金占比 1200/3000=40%，只承担 F1+F2 的 40%。
错误bri_landlord_deduction_share.exact245.5240.0任务要求：输出 Bri 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Bri 押金占比 35%，承担 F1+F2 的 35%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 Bedroom B blinds 的 130。错误：根据 source 应为 245.5，但模型输出为 240.0。Bri 押金占比 35%，承担 F1+F2 的 35%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 Bedroom B blinds 的 130。
错误chen_landlord_deduction_share.exact502.5530.0任务要求：输出 Chen 按合租分摊规则应承担的房东扣款份额。Chen 押金占比 25%，承担 F1+F2 的 25%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 living room wall repair 的 420。错误：根据 source 应为 502.5，但模型输出为 530.0。Chen 押金占比 25%，承担 F1+F2 的 25%，另根据 moveout_inspection_notes_hm37.md 承担 living room wall repair 的 420。
正确alex_utility_adjustment.exact47.447.4任务要求：输出 Alex 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；正数表示多拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv 和 lease rules：U1 电费扣除 Alex EV add-on 后平分，U2/U3 平分，U7 gas 按 12/16 天 prorate 后 108 平分；Alex 已支付 192.60，应承担 181.20，调整额为 11.40。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确bri_utility_adjustment.exact-34.2-34.2任务要求：输出 Bri 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；负数表示少拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv：Bri 支付网络 75，应承担 U1/U2/U3/U7 合计 145.20，所以调整额为 75-145.20=-70.20。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
正确chen_utility_adjustment.exact-13.2-13.2任务要求：输出 Chen 的净水电网调整额，即已支付纳入结算的水电网金额减去自己应承担金额；负数表示少拿回。final_utilities_hm37.csv：Chen 支付水费/垃圾费 96，并对 U7 只计入 prorated 108，纳入支付合计 204，应承担 145.20，所以调整额为 58.80。正确：模型输出与该检查项的 source 依据一致。
错误alex_final_refund.exact1115.41137.4任务要求：输出 Alex 的有效押金出资减去房东扣款份额，再加水电网调整额后的最终退款。Alex 押金基数为 1200-132=1068，再加 utility adjustment 11.40，最终 1079.40。错误：根据 source 应为 1115.4，但模型输出为 1137.4。Alex 押金基数为 1200-132=1068，再加 utility adjustment 11.40，最终 1079.40。
错误bri_final_refund.exact770.3775.8任务要求：输出 Bri 的有效押金出资减去房东扣款份额，再加水电网调整额后的最终退款。Bri 押金基数为 1050-245.50=804.50，再加 utility adjustment -70.20，最终 734.30。错误：根据 source 应为 770.3，但模型输出为 775.8。Bri 押金基数为 1050-245.50=804.50，再加 utility adjustment -70.20，最终 734.30。
